# better-supabase

## 0.9.0

### Minor Changes

- [#192](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/192) [`e2bc289`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/e2bc289066841eafd9335ac9b5850a8a3f5a3408) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `better-supabase adopt <block> --from <schema.table>` maps a block onto tables the app already has. It reads the tables like `introspect`, matches the block's logical tables and columns to them (including likely renames such as `organization_id` for `tenant`, and `null` for optional columns the table lacks), prints the mapping with a doctor-style report of what can't map, and writes `blocks.<block>` in adopt mode when everything maps. `--dry-run` writes nothing and `--json` prints the entry, the mapping and the report.
  
  `apply --preset <code> --adopt` proposes the same mapping for every block of the preset that has a table under its default names, shows it before the config diff, and writes nothing when one of those tables can't be adopted. `apply` now also takes `--snapshot`, `--db-url-stdin` and `--project-ref`, and `--json` prints the config it writes and the blocks it adopted.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`81673c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/81673c1886e7a4189f15f3628fbc114a885ba4b6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - An RFC 8693 `act` level may be `{ kind: "agent", sub, tools }`. `agentClaims(claims, { agent, tools })` from `better-supabase/server` builds it on top of the user's claims, and agent runs carry it as `run.act`. An agent writes with the user's row level security unless its level sets `read_only: true`, and a `scope` on its token limits it like an OAuth client. The read-only check in every SQL module knows the new kind, so `sql sync` rewrites each installed module.
  
  `inboxAdapter({ identities })` resolves each inbox contact before the handlers run and issues a run ticket for a contact linked to a signed-in user, which `ticketOf(message)` returns for `forContext({ ticket })`. `testAuthorizationHooks` also checks `automation.mayPublish` and that a support start cannot approve itself.

- [#161](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/161) [`e91b0a5`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/e91b0a5ea7cd01d58f4d125ee322fb68c5ff831a) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `createAgentRuntime` from `better-supabase/ai-sdk/agents` takes `caller` (the invoking user, always the principal; the agent is only the actor), `authorizeModel` (checked before every model call, refusing with `forbidden` before tokens are spent), `results` (filters knowledge hits before the model sees them), `operations`, `permissions` and `authorizeTool` (each tool is the operation `ai.tool.<name>`, the provider's entry wins, and `high` risk waits for approval), and `gateway` (AI Gateway `user` and tags from the caller). `searchTool` and `recallTool` take `results` too. Breaking: `gatewayOptions` drops a `user` passed in its second argument, so `user` only ever comes from `context.userId`; pass the caller's id there. `GatewayContext` takes `tags`.

- [#149](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/149) [`4c822b7`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/4c822b7e62df50175857c4b42791fae34ac90ccb) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - AI chat sharing moves onto the `share-links` block. `share_ai_chat`, `revoke_ai_chat_share`, `list_ai_chat_shares` and `get_shared_ai_chat` stay callable and use `share_links`. Existing `ai_chat_shares` rows are copied, including the leaf. New links are stored only as a hash.

- [#206](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/206) [`eb98b92`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/eb98b926a0506c624c8345dc8c5c142fb4cc86c8) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - API keys have a `kind`. List the allowed kinds in `blocks.api-keys.options.kinds` (a check constraint enforces them) and pass `kind` to `create`. `create_api_key` takes `kind` as its last argument; the module's upgrade drops the old signature. `apiKeyResolver`, `apiKeyClaims` and `withApiKey` take a `claims(key)` hook that adds claims but cannot set `sub`, `role`, `aud` or `api_key`, and `serviceRoles` and `claims` receive the key with the `fields` columns.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`81673c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/81673c1886e7a4189f15f3628fbc114a885ba4b6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `approval_eligible` also asks the provider's `isApprover` for the kind `<type>.<action>`, and never for the requester. `createApprovals` takes `approverOf`, `wakeOnApproval` from `better-supabase/workflow-sdk` resumes a run waiting on a request id when the request is approved, rejected, expired or cancelled, and a workflow-builder approval node can file an approvals request and wait on its id.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`79e604b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/79e604b7d033c6396df8ae3e8c6f765e917ed4b1) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - With the `approvals` SQL module installed, AI-chat tool calls that wait for approval (`ai_tool_approval`, action `run`) and support access requests for a tenant (`support_request`, action `start`) also file an approval request. Deciding either side decides the other: `decide_approval` approves or denies the tool call and approves or closes the support request, and `decide_ai_tool_approval` or `approve_support_request` settles the approval request. The owning module still decides who may approve, these requests can't be delegated, and `approvals.read` alone doesn't list them, so tool inputs and support reasons stay with their approvers.

- [#210](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/210) [`f2b876b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/f2b876b40af3468085e10893b2c29ed4b8d8e301) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - AsyncAPI channels now come from the same `defineTopic` definitions that `sql sync` turns into Realtime policies. Pass `topicsFrom(module)` (from `better-supabase/asyncapi`) to `defineApi({ topics })`. Each channel gets its broadcast events, its presence schema and an `x-better-supabase-realtime-status` of `beta` for private topics (Realtime Authorization is still in public beta) or `ga` for public ones. A topic gets a send operation only when it sets `clientSend: true`; before, every topic got one. `defineTopic` takes the new `presenceSchema`, `clientSend` and `status` options, and a topic exposes `events`, `clientSend`, `permission` and `status`.
  
  `better-supabase spec` loads the `realtime.policies.from` modules with the loader `sql sync` uses. It warns `asyncapi-topic-undocumented` for each topic the AsyncAPI document leaves out, and `--watch` renders again when a topic module changes.
  
  The new `dataApi` option documents the Supabase Data API next to your routes: `/rest/v1/<table>` operations and `/rest/v1/rpc/<function>` calls, with database column names, the API key and JWT schemes, the PostgREST error body and their own `servers` from `supabaseUrl`. Operations take an optional `servers` list, which the OpenAPI formats render. New diagnostics: `data-api-server-missing`, `data-api-table-unknown` and `data-api-function-unknown`.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`6e9455f`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/6e9455f375640821e56aea1daa1ff1459baf5984) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The audit block reverts row changes: `audit.revert(entryIds)` (SQL `revert_audit_entries`) writes the old values back as the caller, newest entry first, all or nothing, and refuses with `ROW_CHANGED_SINCE` when a row changed after the entry. Members need the new `audit.revert` permission, which admins have by default. The result carries an undo token that reverts the revert. The saved-views read policy now evaluates `auth.uid()` once per query when the teams module is installed.

- [#208](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/208) [`63e5b0d`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/63e5b0d96ace0ed756aadebe9f8b83c91874de1d) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Fixes from an audit of the blocks, the jobs worker, the commerce adapters and the CLI.
  
  Breaking: `fromStripeAmount` returns a `Result<Money>`. It refuses an amount that isn't a safe integer, or an ISK or UGX amount with a fraction of the minor unit, with `invalid_input` and the hint `STRIPE_AMOUNT_PRECISION`, where it used to truncate. The Stripe payments and tax adapters return that error instead of a wrong amount.
  
  The notifications and settings blocks split their own-row `for all` policies into read, insert, update and delete policies, so `actor_may_write()` guards writes only and a read-only support session can still read. Run `better-supabase sql sync` and generate a migration.
  
  - The data-lifecycle purge checks the legal hold and the due date before it deletes storage objects or calls a webhook.
  - A jobs worker aborts a job's signal when its lease can't be extended, and reports `JOB_LEASE_LOST` through `onError` instead of completing a job another worker may hold. A failed `complete` is reported too.
  - The idempotency middleware answers `409` with `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_LOST` when the lock expired before the response was saved, and the request fingerprint includes the query string.
  - The webhook inbox passes its handler an `AbortSignal` that aborts when the lease can't be renewed, and then leaves the message to the next worker.
  - The outbox treats a handler that returns a failed `Result` as a failure, so the event is retried.
  - `deleteAccount` succeeds on a retry for a user that Auth already deleted, and still removes the listed storage objects.
  - Invoice deposits are computed in `numeric`, so line totals round half away from zero past 2^53.
  - The search block adds a partial index for rows waiting for an embedding.
  - `env --out`, `env.output` and `env --from` refuse a path outside the project root.
  - A `42501` error says "permission denied" to the client, and keeps the object name and PostgREST's grant recipe in `details`.
  - `queueBackend({ client, schema })` calls the wrappers `blocks.jobs.api` writes into that schema, for the full feature set over the Data API. `client` alone still uses the `pgmq_public` RPCs.
  - `supabaseCollection` from `better-supabase/tanstack-db/supabase` works with `casing: 'camel'` and with tables outside `public`: it renames filters, bodies, rows and Realtime payloads, and runs requests and Realtime listeners in the table's schema.
  - A subject's `deletedAt` column becomes the table's `softDelete` flag in `gen` (it wins over `plugins.softDelete.column`), and declared metrics skip those rows unless the metric sets `includeDeleted: true`.

- [#161](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/161) [`15ee010`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/15ee0107325e38dd993bdef378abfdd92a873277) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `createServer(betterSupabase, { authorization })` takes `AuthorizationHooks` (`defineAuthorizationHooks` from `better-supabase/server`): optional `automation`, `support`, `delivery`, `identities`, `chat`, `approverOf` and `tickets` hooks, so one authorization provider decides publishing, support sessions, fan-out, identity links, chat actions, approvals and run tickets. `testAuthorizationHooks` from `better-supabase/testing` checks that they fail closed.
  
  The `authorization` config gains `functions.isApprover` (`{kind}`, `{subject}`, `{tenant}`), `functions.permitted` for checks on one instance, and `operations`, one table of the permission each workflow node, MCP tool, AI tool and inbox command needs.
  
  Breaking: with `authorization` set, `blocks.access.model` defaults to `provider`, so `can()`, `tenant_ids_with()` and every module check answer from the provider. Set `model: "roles"` to keep the built-in roles; doctor BS414 then reports policies and functions that call the access contract. `functions.canApprove` is deprecated in favour of `isApprover` and still read when `isApprover` is unset; doctor BS415 warns while a provider sets it, and `better-supabase codemod 0.9` lists each use.

- [#197](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/197) [`5be540b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/5be540bc73d91caac98112ee060a0b61fadddab0) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add client controllers and hooks for the `billing`, `usage`, `entitlements` and `settings` blocks, each with `/react`, `/vue`, `/svelte` and `/solid` entries. `usePlanPicker()` (`better-supabase/blocks/billing/react`) lists your plans with the current one and starts Checkout through a server action you pass, so Stripe is only called on the server. `useUsageMeter()` (`better-supabase/blocks/usage/react`) reads this period's usage per meter with its fraction of the quota. `useFeatureGate()` (`better-supabase/blocks/entitlements/react`) tells whether the active organization's plan includes a feature from the session's features claim, and `hasEntitlement()` now also takes the client's `useAuth()` snapshot. `useSettings()` (`better-supabase/blocks/settings/react`) edits one scope of a `defineSettings()` definition with `fields` generated from each key's Standard JSON Schema, and `setField()` for generated forms. The plan picker, usage meter, feature gate and settings layout recipes show them in the Next.js and Expo examples.

- [#200](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/200) [`bfdcf8a`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/bfdcf8acd576ac6bce3de8eff855c841f90a3dd2) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - More blocks meet the block contract. `createAuditLog` is now `createAudit`. The clients of agents, ai-cache, ai-chat, ai-files, ai-providers, ai-tasks, announcements, api-keys, attachments, audit, billing, bookings, bulk, calendar, comments, connectors and custom-fields take `hooks` (and `logger`) and run them around every method, and audit's purge calls and `activitySink` go through `blockCall`, so error mappers apply.
  
  Access-requests, agents, ai-chat, ai-files, ai-tasks, announcements, api-keys, approvals, attachments, bookings, calendar, catalog and collections take `options.extraColumns` on their main table and a `fields` Standard Schema on the client. The module adds the columns, its writes take them (columns a write leaves out keep their value or default) and its reads return them under `fields`; the client validates them on writes, parses them on reads and merges them into the rows it returns. Without `extraColumns` the SQL is unchanged.

- [#205](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/205) [`c96d3d2`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/c96d3d2b3429db6ed634d2b9e9327c253425683c) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `comments`, `connectors` and `rate-limit` follow the block contract.
  
  `createRateLimit` takes one options object: `createRateLimit({ sql: postgres.admin })` or `createRateLimit({ transport: rpcTransport(adminClient) })` replaces `createRateLimit(source, options)`, with `schema`, `mappers`, `hooks`, `logger` and `temporal` next to it. `createBlocks` builds the `rate-limit` block from its shared `transport`.
  
  `comments` and `connectors` take `options.extraColumns` (on `comments` and `connector_servers`) and a Standard Schema `fields` option on `createComments` and `createConnectors`. `create` and `edit` of comments, and `servers.create` and `servers.update` of connectors, validate the `fields` they get and write them; every comment and server read returns the columns parsed under `fields`, and `comments.copy` copies them. `create_comment` and `edit_comment` take a `fields jsonb` argument only when extra columns are configured; `save_connector_server` takes them in its existing `fields` object.
  
  Every SQL module that takes a `fields jsonb` argument for its extra columns now names it `fields`: `request_access`, `reserve_ai_file`, `store_ai_file`, `create_api_key`, `request_approval`, `create_attachment`, `save_product`, `create_collection` and `rename_collection` took `extra_fields` before. Each module drops the signature it doesn't create, so turning `extraColumns` on or off never leaves two overloads a call matches, and the module contracts list the argument when it exists.

- [#201](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/201) [`48da041`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/48da0412ab51ef987a59112905bc442e37228df6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Blocks from `notifications` to `settings` follow the block contract.
  
  `onboarding`, `outbox` and `settings` have a `create<Name>(options)` creator, and their manifests build it. `createOnboarding({ transport })` replaces `checklist.connect(...)`: its methods take the checklist first (`onboarding.complete(gettingStarted, "invite", organizationId)`), and `createOnboardingController` takes `client` and `checklist` instead of `scope`. `createSettings({ settings, transport })` replaces `settings.connect(...)`, with flat methods (`getUser`, `setUser`, `resetUser`, `getOrganization`, `setOrganization`, `resetOrganization`, `getPlatform`, `setPlatform`, `resetPlatform`); `defineSettings` returns only the `schemas`. `createOutbox({ transport, source })` replaces `createOutbox(source, options)`: pass `sqlTransport(postgres.admin)` for a server connection, and `service` for the consumer calls when `transport` acts as a user.
  
  `hooks` (and `logger`) now work on `createNumberSequences`, `createPush`, `createRank`, `createRateLimit`, `createRelations`, `createReminders`, `createSavedViews`, `createServicePrincipals`, `createOnboarding`, `createSettings` and `createOutbox`; job handlers call their methods through the hooks. `createNotifications` sends its delivery calls through `blockCall`, so `deliver()` rejects with the mapped `DbError`.
  
  `options.extraColumns` and a typed `fields` option cover the main table of `invitations` (as `invitationFields` on `createOrganizations`), `notifications`, `orders`, `pipelines`, `promotions`, `push`, `relations`, `reminders`, `saved-views` and `service-principals`, and `fields` now types the columns `parties` and `quotes` already took. Writes take `fields` and validate them before the call; reads return them in `fields`, built with `jsonb_build_object`, and parse them with the schema. Without `extraColumns` the SQL is unchanged.

- [#187](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/187) [`ed3459b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/ed3459b309817681d1705c79047320a30c202004) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The 14 blocks with a headless controller (ai-chat, announcements, bookings, calendar, forms, inbox, notifications, onboarding, reactions, saved-views, search, undo, workflows and workflow-builder) get `better-supabase/blocks/<name>/vue`, `/svelte` and `/solid` entries. Each exports the React hook names (`useInbox`, `useUndoStack` and so on) over the same controllers: Vue returns a shallow ref of the snapshot, Svelte a readable store and Solid an accessor from `from`, with the controller's actions next to it. They take the controller's options; `auth` and `ports` come from `betterSupabase(bs)`, `setBetterSupabase(bs)` or `<BetterSupabaseProvider>` unless you pass them. The work starts on mount, never during server rendering, and the controller is disposed with the component.
  
  `testControllerBinding` from `better-supabase/testing` checks that a framework binding renders a controller's snapshot, follows its changes, passes its actions through and stops it on unmount. The React, Vue, Svelte and Solid bindings run it.

- [#188](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/188) [`a678150`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/a67815042c82b650e52d5f226251e82d95650899) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Every block exports a manifest built with `defineBlock` (`commentsBlock` from `better-supabase/blocks/comments`): its SQL modules, option keys, its own events, the ports it provides and consumes, its creator, category and UI recipes. `createBlocks(options, blocks)` takes manifests and wires them on their ports (`notifier`, `files`, `attachments`, `workflows`, `sink`) instead of on names. `block.with(options)` sets a block's own options, and `block.using(create)` builds it with your own call to its creator. `defineBlocks(blocks, ...appEvents)` types the events of every block and the app's.
  
  Breaking:
  
  - `createBlocks` no longer takes factories or the `aiTaskNotification`, `reminderNotification`, `metricEvents` and `metricTime` options. Use `aiTasksBlock.with({ notification })`, `remindersBlock.with({ notification })` and `metricsBlock.with({ sink: { on, time } })`.
  - The closed `BlockEventMap` is gone. Each block exports its own event map (`CommentsEvents` and `commentsEvents`), `onBlockEvent(betterSupabase, events, pattern, handler)` takes the events to type by, `blockEmitter`, `emitBlockEvent`, `BlockEvent`, `BlockEventSchemas` and `SubjectEventType` take an event map, and `defineEvents` declares app events.
  - `createDrive` takes the attachments block as `attachments` instead of building one (`attachmentsSchema`, `requireScan` and `downloadTtl` move to that block), and the workflow builder takes the workflows block as `workflows` for schedule triggers.
  - `createBuilder` is renamed `createWorkflowBuilder` and `createPushDevices` is renamed `createPush`.

- [#188](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/188) [`a678150`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/a67815042c82b650e52d5f226251e82d95650899) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Blocks are configured under a top-level `blocks` key in `better-supabase.config.ts`, the foundation blocks (`tenant`, `access`, `updated-at`, `ensure-rls`, ...) included. `sql.modules` is removed; `sql` keeps the SQL-wide settings (`workflow`, `dir`, `prefix`, `testsDir`, `schema`, `api`). The resolved config carries `blocks` and `blockNames`, `sql add` edits `blocks`, and `schemas/config-v1.json` describes the new key. The config types are renamed to match: `BlocksConfig`, `BlockConfig`, `AccessBlockConfig` and `BlockMode`.

- [#198](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/198) [`db9de98`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/db9de9877b314cc9733a6d8b598fdc2b52916870) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - More blocks meet the block contract. `createFlagAdmin` is now `createFlags`. `createJobs({ queues, sql })` replaces `createJobs(source, queues, options)`, taking its store as `backend`, `sql`, `client` or `transport`, and the jobs block is built by `createBlocks`. `createIdempotency({ sql })` and `createIncomingWebhooks({ sql })` take their connection as an option, and the entitlements block gets `createEntitlements` with `has` and `value`.
  
  Deals, drive, employees, engagements, flags, forms, hiring, inventory and invoices take `options.extraColumns` on their main table and a Standard Schema as `fields`, which types and parses the columns on reads and validates them on writes. `create_form`, `update_form` and `save_location` take a `fields` argument. Data-lifecycle, entitlements, flags, idempotency, identities, imports, inbox, incoming webhooks, jobs, knowledge and memory accept `hooks`; inbox, knowledge and memory also take hooks per method group. Calls over a transport go through `blockCall`, so `mappers` apply to them.

- [#195](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/195) [`5677747`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/5677747e63dab922c5b458da0d53669340b0363d) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - SQL module comments and error messages name the `blocks` config key instead of the old `modules` key, for example `blocks["support-sessions"].options.allowWrites` in the `SUPPORT_WRITES_DISABLED` error.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`b2143fb`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/b2143fb777ec22b569f1b6cfd120264cdcd973ca) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the bookings block (`better-supabase/blocks/bookings` and `better-supabase/blocks/bookings/react`): bookable resources with weekly hours, exceptions and a capacity, services with a duration and buffers, open slots from the `available_slots` SQL function, holds that a `bookings.expire-holds` job cancels, an exclusion constraint that refuses double bookings (the module installs `btree_gist`), public booking through a share link with a rate limit and an access token, confirmed bookings copied into the calendar block, customer reminders, and a `useAvailableSlots` hook typed for headless time pickers such as ReUI's.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`991058e`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/991058e201fb59fe6233657a518a765ae9def0dd) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The bulk module adds a `trash.trash` action when the trash module is installed, so a list's bulk bar can move many rows to the trash in one call or a background run, each row through the trash block's own checks.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`2e0fa4a`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/2e0fa4a2814e57c949a501224a383da49de2c87a) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add bulk actions (`better-supabase/blocks/bulk` and the `bulk` SQL module). `bulk.run` applies `tags.set`, `stateMachine.transition`, `relations.link` or an app's own action to up to 500 rows in one call with a result per row, and `bulk.start` puts larger sets on the jobs queue `bulk`, where each step runs as the user who started the run and broadcasts its progress on a Realtime topic.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`d3846d1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/d3846d10a9bf9800fcf3125c9cfe5d3a66ea6f77) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Adds the calendar block (`better-supabase/blocks/calendar` and `better-supabase/blocks/calendar/react`, SQL module `calendar`). It stores calendars, events with RFC 5545 recurrence, overrides and attendees. `occurrences()` expands series with the reminders block's `expandRule`, `editOccurrence` and `editFollowing` override one occurrence or split a series, `importIcs` and `exportIcs` read and write iCalendar files, and a share link to a calendar serves an iCal feed. `useCalendar` returns events in the shape an event calendar renders.
  
  `expandRule` takes a `window` so long-running series expand only the occurrences in a range, and `revoke_share_link` no longer fails with an ambiguous `id` column.

- [#148](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/148) [`1d622d1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/1d622d15bc4014df94aafd89c8284fc8386e0830) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Close the gaps CentraKit hit while adopting better-supabase:
  
  - Support sessions work with an app-owned request context, and the proxy prunes stale cookie chunks and takes an auth-outage hook.
  - `createJobs`, `createIdempotency` and `withLease` accept a block transport (`rpcTransport`), so job stats, dead jobs, retries, idempotency keys and leases work through the `blocks.<name>.api` wrappers without a direct Postgres connection.
  - Jobs, idempotency, rate limits, outgoing webhooks and usage get TypeScript `purge` methods and `purgeJob` handlers for the `purge_*` functions; `purgeAuditLog` and the webhook inbox purge accept a transport.
  - Behaviour change: `cancel_organization_deletion` asks the access contract for the `cancelDelete` permission (`organization.delete` by default) through the new `member_can_while_deleting()`, instead of hard-coding the `owner` role. With the default roles nothing changes; with an authorization provider, any member the provider grants the key can cancel. An access module in `custom` mode keeps the owner check.
  - Attachments take `subjects.<type>.permissions` (`read`, `upload`, `manage`), and attachments and comments take `subjects.<type>.scope`: a scope the authorization provider declares, so a grant on the subject record counts next to one on the tenant. Comments' `readableBy` also takes `{ sql, enforce: true }`, which applies it to the read policy.
  - The `realtime-tables` module adds `better_supabase.broadcast_topic()`, a row trigger that fills a topic's `{column}` placeholders from the row and sends the change. `topic.trigger(table, options)` returns the `create trigger` statement, and `realtime.triggers` makes `sql sync` write them next to the topic policies.
  - Incoming webhooks hand verified deliveries to an `IncomingDestination` (`apiVersion: 1`, checked by `testIncomingDestination`): `inboxDestination` stores them in the webhook inbox as before, and `jobsDestination` enqueues them on a jobs queue once per endpoint and message id, with the endpoint's tenant and metadata. `jobs.enqueueOnce` (`enqueue_job_once` in SQL) is the queue side: a key stays taken after its job completes.
  - `webhooks-in` and `webhook-inbox` gain `mode: "adopt"` over an app's own endpoint and message tables, with renamed columns and optional ones mapped to `null`. Their functions stay in `better_supabase`. In managed mode a column mapping is an error.
  - `credentials` gains `mode: "adopt"` over an app's own secrets table (`provider`, `name` and optional `tenant`, `scope`, `vaultId`, `secret`, `updatedAt` columns), with `secretStorage: "vault"` (a Vault id per row, deleted with its row) or `"column"`. `credential_get`, `credential_set` and `credential_delete` keep their signatures, so `vaultCredentials()` reads the table unchanged; a `vault` ref names another provider's row as `<provider>:<name>`. Doctor reports a table or column an adopt-mode module maps that the live database lacks (BS330).

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`3e829a2`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/3e829a20c290899844f038792c1b09eabdf3932a) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `access-requests` block (`better-supabase/blocks/access-requests` and the `access-requests` SQL module): members ask for a role, for a set time if they like, and the request goes through the approvals module. The final approval grants the role, `end()` and `expire_access_grants()` put the previous role back, and the module records `access.requested`, `access.request_approved` and `access.ended`. With the `provider` access model the adapter grants the role from the events. Approvals gains an internal `open_approval` that files a request under an explicit policy.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`26b7855`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/26b7855b88d73a5d7808ea786ec7864416ec59e3) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `approvals` block (`better-supabase/blocks/approvals` and the `approvals` SQL module): approval requests with steps per subject type and action, approvers by permission, role, team or user, a required count, delegation, self-approval off by default, expiry and reminders. The final approval runs a state-machine transition or enqueues a job, approvers and requesters are notified through the notifications module, and the module records `approval.*` events. State machines gain `requiresApproval`, which reserves transitions for an approved request (`STATE_APPROVAL_REQUIRED`).

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`30816cc`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/30816cc3a73a642019937646f27288f05a2134f1) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `documents` block (`better-supabase/blocks/documents` and the `documents` SQL module) and the `better-supabase/yjs` provider. A document is a Yjs update log and a compacted snapshot attached to any subject, with a private `documents:<id>` Realtime topic for edits and presence under the subject's read and write rules. `createDocumentProvider` connects a `Y.Doc`: it loads the stored state, stores and broadcasts each edit, and catches up after a reconnect. `compactDocument` merges the log for the `documents.compact` job, and with the versions module a document's state can be saved as a version. `yjs` (`>=13.6 <14`) is a new optional peer.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`1897797`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/18977974f285755f1d3c12a7bb25289c163f8c60) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `reactions` block (`better-supabase/blocks/reactions`, `better-supabase/blocks/reactions/react` and the `reactions` SQL module): emoji reactions on any registry subject, comments and inbox messages, checked against each subject's own read policies, with counts, `reacted` and the first actors per emoji, custom emoji per organization, a `reactions:{organizationId}` realtime topic and `useReactions()`. The module records `reaction.added` and `reaction.removed`. With it installed, the inbox's `react_to_message` stores reactions in the table and keeps the message's `reactions` column as a copy; installing it copies existing message reactions.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`d29f784`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/d29f784df80fde8d57222041eeb830783146658f) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `trash` block (`better-supabase/blocks/trash` and the `trash` SQL module): soft delete through each subject's `deletedAt` column, children trashed in the same batch, restore with unique column checks, a list per organization, `purge()`, `purge_expired_trash()` after `retentionDays`, and `is_trashed()` for policies. The module records `subject.trashed`, `subject.restored` and `subject.purged`.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`c401156`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/c40115607910a057dcf6f079166d28a424d50394) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `versions` block (`better-supabase/blocks/versions` and the `versions` SQL module): numbered snapshots of any subject with a content hash, labels, debounced auto snapshots, versions frozen when a status column changes, retention, restore through columns or an apply function, and `diff()` as an RFC 6902 JSON Patch. `createPatch()` and `applyPatch()` are exported for any JSON value. The module records `version.created`, `version.restored` and `version.deleted`.

- [#179](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/179) [`5e4b59a`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/5e4b59a61fe3fe34850c8b2973bce780b73eb6b2) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `collections` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/collections` add favorites and lists on any record registered in `config.subjects`. Favorites are a member's own stars, ranked for a sidebar (`star`, `unstar`, `starred`, `reorderStar`). Lists are named, static collections of one record type owned by a user, a team or the organization (`create`, `rename`, `archive`, `add`, `remove`, `reorder`, keyset-paged `entries` and `listsOf`); an entry is visible only when the caller can read its record, and deleting a record with `cascade: true` deletes its entries and stars. A saved view can keep to a list through `state.collectionId` and `collections.where(id)`, and the bulk module gains the `collections.add` action.

- [#165](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/165) [`64ed285`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/64ed285e440fee00a3cb07dcf8ac93e73366d942) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The comments module (version 4) filters activity per record when the `subjects` module is installed: the read policy on `activity_entries` checks `can_subject(subject_type, subject_id, 'read')` for every registered subject type, and `list_activity` returns an empty timeline for a subject the caller can't read. Members no longer see entries on records they can't read, or on deleted records. Entries without a subject, or on a type the registry doesn't list, keep the tenant check alone.
  
  With the audit module installed with `options.readPolicy: true`, a subject's timeline also returns the audit log entries for its row, gated by the audit read policy. `ActivityEntry` has a new `source` field (`ActivitySource`, `"event"` or `"audit"`), so code that builds `ActivityEntry` values by hand needs to set it.
  
  With the notifications module installed, the author of a comment and the users it mentions follow the subject as `participating` watchers in `notification_subscriptions`; an existing row, such as `ignore`, is kept. `options.follow: false` turns this off. Run `sql sync` to rewrite the installed module.

- [#191](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/191) [`6f72624`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/6f72624a59802e52fbdc1ed98b82c01aa8243cd4) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add client controllers and hooks for the `comments` and `organizations` blocks. `createCommentsController` and `useComments()` (`better-supabase/blocks/comments/react`, `/vue`, `/svelte` and `/solid`) load one subject's thread and keep it current after `add`, `edit` and `remove`, which return a `Result`. `createOrganizationsController` and `useOrganizations()` (`better-supabase/blocks/organizations/react`, `/vue`, `/svelte` and `/solid`) list the caller's organizations, read the active one from the tenant claim, and switch with `switchTo()`, which refreshes the session when `switch_organization` asks for it. Both hooks run in React DOM and React Native, and the comment thread and organization switcher recipes show them in the Next.js and Expo examples.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`ba781c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/ba781c142894fb020ec33fbf5390b87bef3db62d) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `catalog` block and SQL module (`better-supabase/blocks/catalog`): products with a draft, active and archived status, options and variants with per-organization SKUs, prices in bigint minor units per currency and price list with validity windows, a category tree, manual and rule collections, and an opt-in public storefront. `catalogSubjects()` registers products and variants as subjects. Grant `catalog.read` and `catalog.manage` to the roles that use it.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`7aabaf1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/7aabaf13030c63bf68cbb427154fcabd1c3f8a2e) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add `better-supabase/blocks/commerce`. It holds money as a `bigint` count of the currency's minor units, using ISO 4217 List One (pinned as `SPEC_PINS.iso4217`), with arithmetic, half-to-even rates, allocation, parsing, formatting and JSON. It also defines the versioned `PaymentProvider` and `TaxProvider` interfaces. `stripePayments` implements the first on Stripe PaymentIntents and webhooks. `stripeTax` and `fixedRates` implement the second. `better-supabase/testing` adds their conformance kits, `testPaymentProvider` and `testTaxProvider`.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`9f3f556`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/9f3f5566549621f74a859e081af5b30abc469d43) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the inventory block (`better-supabase/blocks/inventory`) and its `inventory` SQL module: locations, stock levels per catalog variant and location kept by an append-only movement ledger, reservations that expire (lazily and through a service-only `expire_reservations` job), transfers between locations and purchase orders with partial receiving and backorders. It emits `stock.*`, `reservation.*`, `transfer.*` and `purchase_order.*` events, and the catalog refuses to delete variants with stock history.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`a9728a3`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/a9728a368ba61e62ad27684dc796691f964b1ff0) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `orders` block and SQL module (`better-supabase/blocks/orders`). Carts belong to the signed-in user, anonymous users included, and `mergeCarts` moves them to the account the shopper signs in to. `checkout` prices the cart from the catalog, quotes tax through a `TaxProvider`, places the order with a number from `number-sequences`, reserves inventory and creates the payment through a `PaymentProvider` with an idempotency key. Paid orders turn their reservations into sale movements and get an invoice number. Fulfillments, returns and refunds (with a proportional tax reversal) follow, and orders emit `order.*`, `fulfillment.*` and `return.*` subject events. Grant `orders.read` and `orders.manage` to the roles that run the shop.
  
  `sqlTransport` now sends an array of objects as JSON, so `jsonb` arguments that take a list work over it.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`ab5936d`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/ab5936df9e9a2f1b1078803a9c22d097dabce33b) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `promotions` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/promotions`. Promotions take a percentage or a fixed amount off, or ship for free, for the whole cart or for products, categories (with their children) or manual collections. They apply automatically or by code, with single codes or generated batches, a minimum subtotal, a start and end, usage and per-customer limits, stacking rules and a priority. `orders` prices them into `priceCart`, lists the applied promotions, reports a code that doesn't apply as a cart problem, and redeems them at checkout with a row lock; cancelling an order releases its redemptions. Applied promotions in `PricedCart` and `Order` now carry their `kind`, and cart problems carry the `promotionId` and `reason`. `merge_carts` moves the promotion codes of an anonymous cart to the account's cart.

- [#199](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/199) [`1fbdbdf`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/1fbdbdfee698bcc80b65d1206a20b4d8934687b4) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - **Breaking:** share-links, signatures, sla, tags, targets, teams, templates, time-tracking, trash, versions, waitlist, webhook-inbox, work-items and workflow-builder take `options.extraColumns` on their main table and a Standard Schema `fields` option on the creator; reads return the columns parsed under `fields`. share-links, sso, state-machine, tags, teams, undo, usage, waitlist, webhooks, workflow-builder and workflows take `hooks` (grouped by namespace for workflows and workflow-builder). `createWebhookInbox` takes `{ transport, ... }` instead of a SQL client (`createWebhookInbox({ transport: sqlTransport(postgres.admin), source })`), and `receive_webhook`, `claim_webhooks` and `list_webhooks` return jsonb (webhook-inbox module version 7). `shareLinks.list` returns typed links. The money and payment helpers move from `better-supabase/blocks/commerce` to `better-supabase/commerce`.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`191657b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/191657b71aeb24709609645b7ed16cadcd776fe0) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `createBlocks` returns `sinks`: a `searchSink` over the search block, so trashed, purged and restored subjects update the index, and a `metricsSink` over the metrics block for the events in the metrics block's `sink` option. Pass them to `outbox.relayRoute({ consumers: { ...app.sinks } })`.

- [#168](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/168) [`87edc51`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/87edc51f5831475425cab0b64df5ca28829d47e1) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `deals` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/deals` add deals on pipelines. Installing deals binds the `deal` type in the pipelines module with a Qualified to Closed won or Closed lost ladder. A deal has an amount in minor units, a company and contacts from `config.subjects`, a team, line items, a motion, a priority, a forecast call, flags and a next step. `update(changes)` applies grid edits one by one and returns the refused ones with their hint. The table refuses edits to a locked deal, keeps the open date, counts close-date slips and stamps `closedAt` with `deal.closed`. `win`, `lose` and `reopen` move a deal through its pipeline's stages, `forecast` returns won, commit, best case, pipeline, not called and later with attainment and gap, and `health` flags rotting, slipped, missing and overdue next steps.
  
  Line items share one shape across blocks: `set_<record>_lines` replaces a record's lines and a trigger keeps its list, discount, subtotal, tax and total.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`81673c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/81673c1886e7a4189f15f3628fbc114a885ba4b6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Delegated tokens are narrowed inside the access functions. A token with a `client_id`, or an `act` chain that is not a support or impersonation session, passes `member_can`, `member_can_while_deleting`, `platform_can` and `tenant_ids_with` only for the permission keys its scopes map to, through the new `better_supabase.cap_allows(permission)`. Map scopes in `blocks.access.scopes` or `authorization.delegation.scopes` (a scope without an entry grants the key with its own name, `["*"]` grants every key). `forContext(context, { permissions })` sets a `bs_permission_cap` claim that caps the work the same way, and a provider can map scopes at runtime, per client or from RFC 9396 `authorization_details`, with the `delegation.permissions` hook.
  
  Breaking: an OAuth client or MCP server whose scopes map to no permission key is now refused where it used to pass with the user's roles. The 0.8 to 0.9 guide lists the mapping to add.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`81673c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/81673c1886e7a4189f15f3628fbc114a885ba4b6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Delivery fails closed on records under an authorization provider. Without `delivery.subscribers` or `delivery.audience`, webhooks, notifications and reminders deliver an event about a registered subject only to users and service principals who may read it, through the new `can_subject_for(user, type, id, action)` in the `subjects` module and `subjectReaders(transport)`. Webhooks check each delivery against its stored subject, and a share link never counts.
  
  Breaking: realtime `fanOut` under a server with authorization hooks and no subscriber policy sends an event with a `subject` to no one and returns `forbidden` with the hint `REALTIME_SUBSCRIBER_POLICY_REQUIRED`; pass `policy: subjectReaders(transport)`.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`1977848`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/1977848bd7ff4ff064bee01eba694ab3d241b574) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Adds the drive block (`sql add drive`, `better-supabase/blocks/drive`): folders and files on attachments, with grants to users, teams or roles that apply to everything under a folder. The same check runs in the drive functions, the attachment policies and restrictive `storage.objects` policies. Moves and renames follow a name-conflict policy. With versions, trash and share-links installed, the drive also keeps revisions, a bin, starred and recent items and folder share links. `driveTree` shapes nodes for a tree view, and `driveSubject()` lets other blocks attach to nodes. Adopt mode runs the drive on an existing tree and grant table. A SQL module can now set `before` to render ahead of another module without renumbering the generated files, and `drive_share` takes a team id from the teams table, so an adopted uuid team column accepts it.

- [#177](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/177) [`be6fd6b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/be6fd6b11318edb44e62e7516dce75d5e8699036) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `employees` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/employees` add the people an organization employs, with or without an account. An employee has a manager, team, job title, employment type, start and end date, and a status of preboarding, active, on leave or offboarded; the database refuses a manager who reports to the employee. `update` applies batched cell edits, `orgChart` and `reportsOf` read the tree (managers read their own reports), and `offboard` sets the end date and moves the reports up. `fromApplication` makes one employee from a hired application of the hiring block. Private details and a contract history with salaries in minor units sit behind `employees.view_sensitive`, declared to `data-lifecycle` for exports and anonymize rules. `employeesSubjects()` registers employees in `config.subjects`.

- [#167](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/167) [`739094c`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/739094c28b1a05f4e88fe16783bad5a994e98db7) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `engagements` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/engagements` log calls, emails, meetings, notes and tasks on one or more records registered in `config.subjects`, with an owner, a due time, an outcome and an external id that makes a repeat log an update. `of(subject)` reads a record's engagements, `next(subject)` its next planned step with an overdue flag, and `open(organizationId)` a member's open tasks. An engagement is visible to callers who can read one of its records, and assigning it to someone else notifies them.

- [#184](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/184) [`8bb0f16`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/8bb0f164b3a6d023a81767f3ff8c47c6256ed1da) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `entitlements` module reads the Stripe customer from the `billing` or `organizations` module through their configured names: a renamed `billing_customers` table, its `customer` and `tenant` columns, or an `organizations` table in another schema or with another id column now resolve correctly instead of falling back to `stripe_customer_id`, `organization_id` and `better_supabase.organizations`. `entitlements` lists both modules in `integrates`.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`5d7fbe9`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/5d7fbe96eba10a92c64edd1cd21f146e61788aa6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `forms` block (`better-supabase/blocks/forms`). A form has numbered versions of steps and fields with `showIf` conditions, and each version stores a JSON Schema 2020-12 that `pg_jsonschema` checks on every submission. Submissions can be saved as drafts, linked to a record, or turned into one by a handler per form key. With `share-links`, a `respond` link opens the form to anyone who holds it, rate limited per caller with `rate-limit`, and `before_public_form_submission` receives a bot-check token. `useQuestionnaire` from `better-supabase/blocks/forms/react` drives a multi-step form from a version, and `validateAnswers` checks answers in the browser with the same schema.

- [#185](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/185) [`c2fc0fc`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/c2fc0fc1f2a92340dcfd28870c190c2e68197bc8) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Block hooks now wrap headless controllers, so they run in React DOM and React Native alike. Each block with a `/react` entry exports `create<Name>Controller` from its main entry (`createNotificationsController`, `createCommandSearchController` and the rest), with `subscribe`, `getSnapshot`, `actions` and `dispose`; the hooks are `useSyncExternalStore` wrappers over them.
  
  Controllers reach the platform only through ports on the client, `bs.ports`: realtime topics (`topicPort` from `better-supabase/realtime`), storage, the keyboard, file picking and upload, push tokens and deep links. `createClient` from `better-supabase/client` fills the web ports (`webStorage`, `webKeyboard`, `webFilePicker`, `webDeepLinks`, `storageUpload`, and `webPushToken` on request), and `nativePorts()` from `better-supabase/client/native` builds the React Native ones from the Expo modules you pass to `createNativeClient(betterSupabase, supabase, { ports })`.
  
  Breaking changes: `useCommandSearch` listens for Cmd+K through the client's keyboard port instead of `window`, so React Native has no shortcut. `useInboxWidget` keeps the conversation id in the storage port instead of `localStorage`, and reads it after the first render. `groupHits` and its types moved from `blocks/search/react/groups.ts` to `blocks/search/groups.ts` (still exported from `better-supabase/blocks/search/react`).

- [#173](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/173) [`d9ab197`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/d9ab1979ec77778bd45ee593e335971ea2e4ae51) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `hiring` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/hiring` add an applicant tracking system on pipelines. Installing hiring binds the `application` type in the pipelines module with one pipeline per job (Applied, Screening, Interview, Offer, then Hired, Rejected or Withdrawn). Jobs open and close, a candidate is any record in `config.subjects` (a person by default) and applies once per job, and interviewers score the application at its current stage. `hire`, `reject` and `withdraw` end an application, and `application.hired` carries the candidate for the app to turn into an employee.

- [#204](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/204) [`6b5090a`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/6b5090a49b3989585050b903a097208098d7b08c) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `httpSink` aborts a request after `timeoutMs` (10 seconds by default) instead of waiting on an endpoint that never answers. `doctor` gives up on the splinter download and the Management API advisors after 30 seconds.

- [#161](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/161) [`5141e6c`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/5141e6ce3ae41693923522dc1f9c7a5787bb8b43) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `identities` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/identities`: `identityResolver` maps an email address or chat user to an app user or contact, linked only through a one-time code from `beginLink` that the service redeems with `redeemLink`, or a confirmed `auth.users` address. `better-supabase/chat-sdk` adds `botJobs`, which enqueues a linked author's job with a ticket and an unlinked author's with none, `chatActions`, which decides buttons through `chat.onAction` and refuses unlinked authors, and `approvalLink` and `approvalPrompt`, which link to the app's approval page without approving.

- [#149](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/149) [`4c822b7`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/4c822b7e62df50175857c4b42791fae34ac90ccb) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The inbox no longer owns `inbox_teams` and `inbox_team_members`. It requires the `teams` block and assigns a conversation to a `teams` row. Installing both copies the old rows and drops the old tables. `create_inbox_team` and `set_inbox_team_member` call the teams functions. Team ids stay `uuid` while the inbox is installed.

- [#161](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/161) [`1092624`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/1092624d3ad98a0af4f4b2a93249c6237a4103f4) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Jobs and workflow runs that an OAuth or MCP client queues keep the client's limits: the envelope records the token's `client_id`, `scope`, `authorization_details` and full RFC 8693 `act` chain, and `bs.forContext` runs with them instead of the user's full rights. The new SQL helper `better_supabase.token_scope_allows(scope)` in the `actor` module checks a delegated token's scope in policies.
  
  `enqueue` fails with `forbidden` for a context with an invalid `act` chain or malformed delegation claims, and `forContext` refuses such a stored context. `ServerOptions.claimsFor` can no longer set `act`, `client_id`, `scope` or `authorization_details`. A job's `delay` must be a whole number of seconds, else `enqueue` fails with `validation`.
  
  Jobs and workflows take an opaque `ticket`: the `ticket` enqueue option, `startFor(ctx, workflow, args, { ticket })`, `workflowContext(ctx, { ticket })` and `startOnEvent({ ticket })`. Runs carry it as the `bs.ticket` attribute, and `forContext` resolves it through `ForContextOptions.ticket` or the provider's `tickets.subject` hook, failing with `forbidden` when nothing resolves it. `startOnEvent`'s `args` now gets the event's context as a second argument.
  
  `better-supabase/workflow-sdk` exports `approvalWaiter`: `wait(token, { expiresAt })` pauses a run until `wake(token, payload)` or the RFC 3339 deadline.

- [#150](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/150) [`615d098`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/615d098944c1765c3adac32b13fc69ec2c1d9900) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add `filters` to `defineListQuery`: an allow-list of typed fields that a ReUI Filters `FilterQuery` tree may name. `parse` checks each rule's operator and value against the field type, caps depth and rule count, drops unfinished rules, and takes a `timeZone` that relative dates resolve in. The tree compiles to repository `where` input, so it runs through PostgREST, the Postgres executor and SQLite. `filterFields(labels)` returns ReUI `FilterField` objects, the URL keeps the tree in one base64url `filter` parameter that `toSearchParams` and the nuqs parsers handle, and `openapi` and `jsonSchema` describe it.

- [#203](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/203) [`9d8a9d2`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/9d8a9d25023da6abe88e2031fd29ae3c0c2b7d40) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add client controllers and hooks for members, invitations, the audit log, API keys and tags. `useMembers()` and `useInvitations()` (`better-supabase/blocks/organizations/react`) list an organization's members and open invitations; `setRole()` shows a role change at once, `remove()` removes a member, and `invite()` keeps the token for the accept link in `sent`. `useAuditLog()` (`better-supabase/blocks/audit/react`) pages through the audit trail with `setFilters()` by actor, action or text and `loadMore()`. `useApiKeys()` (`better-supabase/blocks/api-keys/react`) lists, creates and revokes keys and shows a new token once. `useTagInput()` (`better-supabase/blocks/tags/react`) adds, removes and creates a record's tags. Each has a `create<Name>Controller` in the block's main entry and `/vue`, `/svelte` and `/solid` bindings, and runs in React DOM and React Native. The tags module gains `list_tags` and `subject_tags`, which `tags.list()` and `tags.forSubject()` call. The members table, invite dialog, API keys table, audit log table and tag input recipes are now available in the Next.js and Expo examples.

- [#160](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/160) [`e99d543`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/e99d543ec60312b21188cfb506b386d59fc3ed31) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Adds the `metrics` block (`better-supabase/blocks/metrics`). `defineMetric` declares a count, sum or average over a table with dimensions, a time zone and a permission. Metrics roll up into `metric_points` per grain through a `metrics.rollup` job or `metricsSink`, or are read from the table directly. `series()` and `kpi()` compare with the previous range, and `toChartData` returns Recharts rows. Usage events can be a metric's source.

- [#206](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/206) [`08b2dbc`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/08b2dbcf83f1d54568693c4317d1b077f7454b4e) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add the `better-supabase/blocks/mfa` and `better-supabase/blocks/sessions` entries, with React, Vue, Svelte and Solid
  bindings. `useMfa` enrols, verifies and removes TOTP factors, and `useStepUp` raises a session to `aal2` after an
  `INSUFFICIENT_AAL` refusal (`needsStepUp`). `useOAuthConsent` serves the consent page of the Auth OAuth server, and
  `useOAuthGrants` lists and revokes the clients a user approved.

- [#165](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/165) [`7c5a97b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/7c5a97be265916d987549a06951563dc66483e61) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The notifications module tracks what an email provider reports after a send. `notification_delivery_events` stores each `delivered`, `opened`, `clicked`, `bounced`, `complained` or `delayed` event once per provider event id, and deliveries gain `provider_status`, `provider_status_at` and `template_key`. `notifications.recordDeliveryEvent(input)` calls the service-only `record_delivery_event`, which moves the status forward, suppresses the address on a hard bounce or a complaint, and emits `notification.<kind>` with the notification's subject. `notifications.subjectDeliveries({ subject })` lists the deliveries of every notification about one record. A `templateKey` returned from the email channel's `compose` is stored on the delivery.
  
  `resendWebhook` now records every Resend delivery status before it writes suppressions, and `resendDeliveryEvent(event, id)` maps one event without the handler. `notification.delivered` and `notification.failed` now carry `subjectType`, `subjectId`, `organizationId` and `deliveryId`, and the five new events are subject events.
  
  `complete_notification_delivery` takes a seventh argument, `template_key`, and the six-argument signature is dropped. Run `better-supabase sql sync` to install module version 6; `resendWebhook` answers 500 against a database without `record_delivery_event`. An adopted module tracks delivery events only when `tables.deliveryEvents` names your table.

- [#149](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/149) [`4c822b7`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/4c822b7e62df50175857c4b42791fae34ac90ccb) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `send` accepts `permission` (with a tenant) and reaches the users who hold that key. A provider supplies them through `authorization.functions.holdersOf`. The roles and catalog models derive holders from memberships. A provider model without `holdersOf` raises `NOTIFICATION_PERMISSION_AUDIENCE_UNAVAILABLE` instead of notifying every member.

- [#161](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/161) [`f48f326`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/f48f32602cef375159e2fb3f038fc88ca92275ed) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `createNotifications` takes `email: { transport, from, compose?, unsubscribe? }`, an email channel over a `DeliveryTransport` (`apiVersion: 1`) that sends with the delivery id as the idempotency key, sets `Auto-Submitted: auto-generated` on all mail and `List-Unsubscribe` with one-click `List-Unsubscribe-Post` on notification mail only. `better-supabase/blocks/notifications` exports `resendTransport` (fetch-based, `Idempotency-Key` header), `resendWebhook` (verifies the Svix signature and records bounces and complaints), `mailHeaders` and `resendSuppressions`, and `better-supabase/testing` gets `testDeliveryTransport`.
  
  The notifications module (version 5) adds `notification_suppressions` with RLS and `suppress_notification_address`, `unsuppress_notification_address` and `list_notification_suppressions`; the block gets `suppress`, `unsuppress` and `suppressions`, and `deliver` skips a suppressed address. `createNotifications({ audience })` checks recipients before sending and falls back to `server.authorization.delivery.audience`. `defineTopic` takes `permission` (an instance permission from a topic segment, checked with the provider's `permitted` template or the access contract) and `subscribers`, and topics get `fanOut`.
  
  Breaking: run `better-supabase sql sync` and create a migration for the module upgrade; an adopted module honours suppressions only when `tables.suppressions` is mapped. `deliver()` counts suppressed and audience-denied deliveries in `skipped`. A hand-written `Topic` object needs `fanOut`.

- [#193](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/193) [`1f41f06`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/1f41f061e3d19f33d7c4e068c76458a9685c6dd8) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - A block's SQL and TypeScript halves now read one config. `better-supabase gen` writes `blocks.generated.ts` next to `generated.ts`: each installed block's schema, API schema and the options both halves share (`attachments.requireScan`, `bulk.limit`, the realtime topics of notifications, announcements, inbox, reactions and ai-chat, `time-tracking.timeZone`, `waitlist.codeField`, `webhooks-out.allowHttp`), with the module defaults filled in. `defineBlocks(blockConfig, blocks, ...appEvents)` takes it as its first argument and passes each block its schema (the API schema when there is one) and those options, so the app no longer repeats them. `createBlocks` takes it as `config`, and its shared `schema` option is removed. `generatedOptions(entry)` gives a creator you call yourself the same values, and `fillTopic(template, values)` fills in a topic template such as `notifications:{userId}`.
  
  `blocks.<name>.options` is typed from each block manifest's options schema: an unknown block, an unknown option or a value of the wrong kind is a type error, and the SQL renderer refuses it too. Manifests describe their options with `blockOptions({ key: option.string(), ... })` in place of `optionKeys([...])`, which is removed, and `schemas/config-v1.json` is generated from the same schemas. `BlocksConfig` is the typed input; the resolved config and the SQL renderer use `AnyBlocksConfig`. Preset wiring (`init --preset`) and the `blocks` registry item build on `defineBlocks(blockConfig, ...)`, and `add` edits a `defineBlocks` call as well as a `createBlocks` one.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`81673c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/81673c1886e7a4189f15f3628fbc114a885ba4b6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Every action a module checks is an operation named `<module>.<action>`, and `better_supabase.operation_permission(op)` returns its permission key in SQL, after `blocks.<module>.permissions` renames and with the provider's `operations` over them. MCP tools take an optional `operation`, agents take `toolOperations`, and workflow-builder step library entries take `operation`, each naming the entry to check in place of `mcp.tool.<name>`, `ai.tool.<name>` or `workflow.run`.

- [#147](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/147) [`919f9be`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/919f9be6aab880d9dfb31738d3a2210f6740ce9f) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `otel()` records a `BATCH` span for the round trip when an executor runs the reads of one `db.$many` call together, as the direct Postgres executor does. The span carries `db.operation.batch.size` and is marked failed when any read fails. Plugins that wrap `executor.batch` now see these batches too.

- [#167](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/167) [`6f561f3`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/6f561f38083c85a3c49fcadb804e104bbc8a6a74) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `parties` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/parties` keep the companies and people an organization works with: lifecycle stages, owners, who works where with a role and one primary contact, dedupe by domain and email, `mergeCompanies` and `mergePeople`, and keyset-paged `listCompanies` and `listPeople`. `partiesSubjects()` registers both tables in `config.subjects` so comments, tags, files and pipelines attach to them, and the owner watches the record when notifications is installed.

- [#175](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/175) [`374df5b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/374df5bf7d8faaf10cb839de90c6e81a4f9311ca) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - With the inbox module installed, the parties module links inbox contacts to people. `personForInboxContact(contactId)` returns the linked person, links the person with the contact's email when there is none, and with `create: true` saves an unknown contact as a new lead. `linkInboxContact` and `unlinkInboxContact` change the link by hand.

- [#167](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/167) [`0c73caf`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/0c73caff1fd9abb0fa831cdadfc06d80d6c76080) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `pipelines` SQL module and `better-supabase/blocks/pipelines` put any record in `config.subjects` on pipelines an organization edits. Bind a type in `blocks.pipelines.options.subjects` with its categories, each mapped to `open`, `success` or `failure`, and the module keeps several pipelines per type (or one per scope, such as a job), checks every stage change, stamps the time a record entered its stage, writes a stage history and records `pipeline.stage_changed` with the record as its subject. A binding can set a status column from the stage category through the state machine, and the owner watches the record when notifications is installed. `createPipelines` manages pipelines, stages and transitions, and reads the board (counts and per-currency totals weighted by probability), stats (entries, time in stage, win rate) and health (days in stage, rotting).

- [#147](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/147) [`a0a6a31`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/a0a6a317ec34594f4a8ddc5a3ad47caaf8700b85) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `createPostgres()` session options take `statementTimeout`, which overrides the role's timeout for `asUser`, `executorFor` and `transaction`. `transaction()` without claims now applies `settings`, `readOnly` and `statementTimeout` too, and an `admin` query with no timeout to set runs without `begin` and `commit`.

- [#207](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/207) [`625fd47`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/625fd476bcdff80c3395e87b12d125536364228d) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - **Breaking:** the CLI uses `@supabase/postgrest-typegen` 0.6.0, and the optional peer now accepts `>=0.6.0 <0.7` instead of `>=0.4.0 <0.5`. Upgrade it with `pnpm add -D @supabase/postgrest-typegen@0.6.0`. `database.types.ts` again matches `supabase gen types` from Supabase CLI 2.121, which writes the same `ComputedFields` keys. View columns are typed with the nullability postgrest-typegen 0.6 reads from the view definition, so run `better-supabase gen` to regenerate.
  
  The `GeneratorMetadata` type gains the optional `rpcFunctions` collection that postgrest-typegen 0.6 derives from `functions`, and the `RpcFunction` types it uses are exported next to it. Snapshots and `gen --metadata` leave it out, and the generator resolves it again from the ordered, stable functions of the generated schemas only.

- [#145](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/145) [`238b8d2`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/238b8d2cf2e55ddcfb30ed742d0b2d8f9fbfd0fc) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `watch` and `useWatch` from `better-supabase/powersync` take `refresh: { everyMs, onReconnect }`, so a read that runs on the executor's server `fallback` reruns without waiting for a local change: on an interval (skipped while PowerSync reports it is offline) and when PowerSync connects again. `PowerSyncDatabaseLike` gains the optional `currentStatus` and `registerListener` members that `refresh` reads.

- [#190](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/190) [`8562868`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/85628685b5943566ef2bfb707a21aabe090ec0bb) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The CLI starts and changes projects from presets. `init --preset <name|code|url>` writes the config with `preset`, the module files of every block it installs and a `lib/blocks.ts` that wires their clients, and prints the registry glue for the framework. `apply --preset` sets `preset` in an existing config (or, with `--only blocks`, lists its blocks; with `--only conventions`, takes the conventions alone), shows the diff and writes the modules. `preset decode` and `preset info` print what a code holds and how the config differs from its preset, and `blocks list`, `blocks view` and `blocks search` read the block catalog.
  
  `add <block...>` now adds blocks: it lists them in `blocks`, writes their modules and what they require, wires their clients into `lib/blocks.ts` and names their registry glue. The framework glue moves to `add --framework <integration,...>`; `add` without arguments still sets up the integrations the config lists. In a terminal, `init` first asks for a preset. `better-supabase/preset` adds `presetApplyCommand`, `presetGlue` and `glueCommand`, and `presetInstallCommand` now returns `init --preset <code>`.

- [#189](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/189) [`850437b`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/850437ba533be8d5ea21a8070814310391f28c8c) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - `better-supabase/preset` describes an app as a preset: a block selection plus its conventions (framework, platforms, casing, id type, scope, tenant name, access model, API schema and integrations). `encodePreset` and `decodePreset` turn one into a short code, and `resolvePreset` returns every block to install with the reason it is there, the soft integrations between them and the conflicts. It reads a generated catalog of the blocks, so it runs in the browser. The named presets are `minimal`, `saas`, `crm`, `projects`, `commerce`, `scheduling`, `ai` and `everything`.
  
  `defineConfig({ preset, blocks })` takes a named preset, a code or a `/create?preset=` URL and expands it into `blocks` and its conventions first; the keys you set win. The docs Recipes section is now Presets, at `/docs/presets`.

- [#162](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/162) [`81673c1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/81673c1886e7a4189f15f3628fbc114a885ba4b6) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `promotions` module calls `before_promotion_activate(promotion uuid)` when you define it, after every write that leaves a promotion active: `set_promotion_active(id, true)` and each `save_promotion` of an active promotion. Raising refuses the change, which holds a promotion for approval. The commerce recipe uses it to hold discounts above 30 percent, and now also indexes products for search and saves order grid views.

- [#171](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/171) [`57708b1`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/57708b16d6aeffa01471d4c78149be374e99829d) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - The `quotes` and `invoices` SQL modules and their blocks add the sales documents after a deal. A quote has lines and totals the database keeps and a revision chain. It is sent as a link the customer opens without an account and answers with accept, reject or changes requested, and it expires after its valid date. `quotes.fromDeal` makes one draft per deal. An invoice gets a gapless number when it is issued and collects payments (a payment reference is recorded once, and a refund larger than the amount paid fails with `INVOICE_REFUND_TOO_LARGE`). Credit notes reduce its balance, and it can be voided, written off and aged per currency. `invoices.fromQuote` makes deposit invoices and one final invoice that subtracts them. With the relations module installed, both link to their origin with `created_from`.

- [#150](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/150) [`c42b9a4`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/c42b9a4d8feef801861aaccb5c5dbb17b550fd45) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add fractional rank keys. `better-supabase/blocks/rank` exports `rankBetween`, `rankKeys`, `isRankKey` and `createRank`, and the new `rank` SQL module adds `rank_between`, `move()` and a `rank.rebalance` job for subjects with a `rank` entry in `config.subjects`. `move()` runs as the caller and can change the row's group, so a state-machine trigger on that column checks the change.
  
  Tags and custom fields now order definitions by a `rank` key instead of an integer `position`. Both modules move to version 2: the upgrade backfills keys in the old order and drops `position`. `tags.reorder` and `customFields.reorderDefinition` take the neighbours' keys. Adopt configs that mapped `position` map `rank` instead. An adopted table without a rank column maps it to `null` (`columns: { tags: { rank: null } }`, and `definitions` and `options` for custom fields); `sql sync` names the column until you do.
  
  A state-machine config without `initial` now names `initial` in its error instead of `column`.

- [#206](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/206) [`17e0a61`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/17e0a6177beb2045e51c091d21613928979363e5) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - Add `rateLimit()` middleware to the Hono and oRPC adapters, built on `createRateLimit`. Responses carry the
  `RateLimit` and `RateLimit-Policy` fields of draft-ietf-httpapi-ratelimit-headers-11, and a refused request gets a
  429 with `Retry-After`. `better-supabase/server` exports `rateLimitHeader()` and `rateLimitPolicyHeader()`.

- [#149](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/pull/149) [`4c822b7`](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/better-supabase/commit/4c822b7e62df50175857c4b42791fae34ac90ccb) Thanks [@martijn00](https://github.com/martijn00)! - A read-only support or impersonation session can no longer write. Every security definer function granted to `authenticated` or `anon` that writes checks `better_supabase.actor_may_write()`, or `authorization.functions.actorMayWrite` when a provider sets it. The same check is added to generated write policies. Writes that succeeded during those sessions in 0.8.0 now fail with `ACTOR_READ_ONLY` (`42501`). `read_only: false`, an agent actor and a signed-in user without `act` still write. Reads are unchanged. `start_support_session`, `end_support_session` and `expire_support_sessions` stay callable so the session can be left.
